INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[ProductoMaterialID], [p0].[MaterialID], [p0].[Porcentaje], [p0].[ProductoID], [m0].[MaterialID] AS [MaterialID0], [m0].[Estado], [m0].[Nombre]
    FROM [ProductoMateriales] AS [p0]
    INNER JOIN [Materiales] AS [m0] ON [p0].[MaterialID] = [m0].[MaterialID]
) AS [t0] ON [p].[ProductoID] = [t0].[ProductoID]
WHERE [p].[Estado] = @__p_0
ORDER BY [p].[ProductoID], [c].[CategoriaPrincipalID], [m].[MarcaID], [t].[TipoProductoID]
